/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CONTENT VISIBILITY & LAYOUT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Ensure content is visible */
main,
article,
.entry-content,
.wp-block-post-content,
.wp-site-blocks > * {
  background: transparent !important;
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

.entry-content > *,
.wp-block-post-content > * {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

img,
figure,
.wp-block-image {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
  display: block !important;
}

.wp-block-paragraph,
.wp-block-heading,
.wp-block-group {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

/* Gap removal */
.wp-site-blocks > * {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}

.entry-content,
.wp-block-post-content {
  max-width: none !important;
  padding-left: 0 !important;
  padding-right: 0 !important;
}

main > *:last-child,
.wp-block-post-content > *:last-child,
.entry-content > *:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
}

/* Full-width blocks */
.wp-site-blocks .alignfull {
  width: 100vw;
  max-width: 100vw;
  margin-left: calc(50% - 50vw);
  margin-right: calc(50% - 50vw);
}

/* Footer spacing */
footer .wp-block-group.alignfull[style*="--wp--preset--spacing--60"],
.wp-block-template-part__footer .wp-block-group.alignfull[style*="--wp--preset--spacing--60"] {
  padding-top: 0 !important;
  padding-bottom: 0.25rem !important;
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}

footer .footer-nav,
.wp-block-template-part__footer .footer-nav {
  margin: 0 auto !important;
  padding-top: 0.25rem !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
  justify-content: center !important;
  gap: 1rem;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SCROLLBAR
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 12px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: #0b0b0f;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.3);
  border-radius: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.5);

---

}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MICRO-ACCENTS & POLISH
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Text selection */
::selection {
  background: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 28%, transparent);
  color: #001a10;
}

/* Input caret */
input, textarea {
  caret-color: var(--brand-mint);
}

/* Focus outlines */
a:focus, button:focus, [tabindex]:focus {
  outline: 2px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 70%, transparent) !important;
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-radius: 6px;
}

/* List bullets */
ul li::marker {
  color: var(--brand-mint);
}

/* Heading underlines */
h2, h3 {
  position: relative;
}

h2::after, h3::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  bottom: -0.45rem;
  width: 64%;
  height: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg,
    transparent 0%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 65%, transparent) 18%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 85%, transparent) 50%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 65%, transparent) 82%,
    transparent 100%);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 45%, transparent));
  opacity: .9;
}

/* Horizontal rules */
hr {
  border: 0;
  height: 2px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 90%, transparent) 45%, transparent 46%) repeat-x;
  background-size: 12px 2px;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 45%, transparent));
  opacity: .85;
}

/* Blockquotes */
blockquote {
  border-left: 3px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 70%, transparent);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-mint) 10%, transparent), transparent 35%);
  padding-left: 1rem !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* Section themes */
.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   WPFORO FORUM
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.wpforo-wrap,
#wpforo-wrap,
.wpforo-main,
#wpforo .wpforo-container,
#wpforo .wpf-container,
.wpforo-forum,
.wpforo-table,
#wpforo #wpforo-wrap,
.wpforo-subtop,
body.wpforo-page .wpf-dark.wpft-forum,
body.wpforo-page #wpforo-wrap,
.wpf-dark.wpft-forum {
  max-width: 100% !important;
  width: 100% !important;
}

#wpforo .wpforo-container,
#wpforo .wpf-container {
  padding-left: 20px !important;
  padding-right: 20px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   IFRAME FIXES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

iframe[src*="base44"] {
  touch-action: manipulation;
  pointer-events: auto;
  -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  -webkit-touch-callout: default;
  -webkit-user-select: auto;

---

user-select: auto;
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  iframe[src*="base44"] {
    width: 100% !important;
    min-height: 70vh;
    border: none;
    z-index: 999;
    position: relative;
  }
}

.mobile-iframe-overlay {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: white;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  z-index: 10;
  pointer-events: none;
}

@media (min-width: 769px) {
  .mobile-iframe-overlay {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* === Unify purple tokens to the lighter shade === */
:root {
  --link-purple: #C3B1E1;                 /* your lighter purple */
  --brand-purple: var(--link-purple) !important; /* make both the same */
}

/* === Header nav: use lighter purple on hover/active === */
header .wp-block-navigation a:hover,
header .wp-block-navigation a:focus,
.nav-top a:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-item > a,
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-ancestor > a {
  color: var(--link-purple) !important;
}

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation a:hover svg,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-item > a svg {
  fill: var(--link-purple) !important;
}

/* === Story nav chips (the pills) === */
.cs-arc-toggle:hover,
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] {
  color: var(--link-purple) !important;
  border-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 65%, transparent) !important;
}

/* If any pill is filled by theme/plugin, normalize it too */
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"],
.cs-arc-toggle.is-active {
  background: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 12%, transparent) !important; /* why: subtle fill */
  box-shadow: none !important;
}

/* One purple to rule them all */
:root {
  --link-purple: #C3B1E1;
  --brand-purple: var(--link-purple);
  /* handy tints from the same token */
  --lp-08: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 8%, transparent);
  --lp-12: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 12%, transparent);
  --lp-15: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 15%, transparent);
  --lp-40: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 40%, transparent);
  --lp-65: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-purple) 65%, transparent);
}

/* Replace scattered RGBA uses with these: 
   background: var(--lp-08|12|15); border-color: var(--lp-15|40|65); */

/* 1) Roll back the accidental group hide on these pages */
body.page-id-945 .wp-block-group,
body.page-id-856 .wp-block-group {
  display: revert !important;      /* why: bring back embed container */
  visibility: revert !important;
}

/* 2) Hide ONLY the page title pieces (safe selectors) */
body.page-id-945 .wp-block-post-title,
body.page-id-856 .wp-block-post-title,
body.page-id-945 .entry-title,
body.page-id-856 .entry-title {
  display: none !important;
}

/* If your theme wraps the title in an entry header, hide that tiny wrapper too */
body.page-id-945 main .entry-header,
body.page-id-856 main .entry-header {
  display: none !important;
}

/* 3) Remove leftover vertical gap under the veil */
body.page-id-945 .wp-site-blocks,
body.page-id-856 .wp-site-blocks {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  padding-top: 44px !important;   /* match your fixed veil height */
}
[1760677533.956165] Assistant: **TL;DR:** Yes—set the iframe height per page. You can use page-scoped CSS vars and override the `iframe[src*="base44"]` height.

### Plan
- Define a veil height token.
- For each page ID, set `--embed-h`.
- Apply to the Base44 iframe (and common WP embed wrappers) with `!important`.
- Use `calc(100vh - var(--veil-h))` if you want it to fill the viewport under the glyph bar.

```css
/* Shared: tokens */
:root { --veil-h: 44px; } /* adjust if your glyph bar is taller */

/* Per-page desired heights (pick what you want) */
body.page-id-945 { --embed-h: calc(100vh - var(--veil-h)); }   /* fill viewport under veil */
body.page-id-856 { --embed-h: 78vh; }                           /* or a fixed vh value */

/* Target the Base44 iframe + common wrappers */
body.page-id-945 iframe[src*="base44"],
body.page-id-945 .wp-block-embed iframe,
body.page-id-945 .wp-block-html iframe,
body.page-id-945 .entry-content iframe[src*="base44"] {
  display: block !important;
  width: 100% !important;
  height: var(--embed-h) !important;
  min-height: 420px !important;  /* safety floor */
  border: 0 !important;
}

body.page-id-856 iframe[src*="base44"],
body.page-id-856 .wp-block-embed iframe,
body.page-id-856 .wp-block-html iframe,
body.page-id-856 .entry-content iframe[src*="base44"] {
  display: block !important;
  width: 100% !important;
  height: var(--embed-h) !important;
  min-height: 420px !important;
  border: 0 !important;
}

/* Optional: mobile tuning */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  body.page-id-945 { --embed-h: calc(100vh - var(--veil-h)); }
  body.page-id-856 { --embed-h: calc(100vh - var(--veil-h)); }
}

/* If a block wrapper sets its own height, normalize it */
body.page-id-945 .wp-block-embed,
body.page-id-856 .wp-block-embed {

---

height: auto !important;
}
```

Notes:
- Cross-origin iframes can’t auto-grow to their internal content height without a JS `postMessage` from the app. The CSS above controls the **viewport slot** height, which is usually what you want.
- If your admin bar is visible, bump `--veil-h` by 32px on `.admin-bar`.

**a.** Want a tiny JS snippet that sets `--embed-h` to `window.innerHeight - veil.offsetHeight` on resize for pixel-perfect fit?  
**b.** Should we give each page its own max-width + centered container so the app card always sits nicely?

/* ───── COLOR TOKENS ───── */
:root {
  --mint: #c4f0e8;
  --mint-strong: #8fffe6;
  --mint-glow: rgba(196,240,232,.4);
  --rose: #ffc7e6;
  --gold: #ffe6a3;
  --indigo: #c5c9ff;

  --cs-purple-hover: #99e9ff;

  --glass-hover: rgba(10,14,20,.78);
  --mint-border: rgba(169,255,232,.5);
  --stroke: rgba(0,0,0,.4);
  --ink: rgba(0,0,0,.5);
  --glow: rgba(255,255,255,.18);

--cs-accent: #7ac9e0;
  --cs-accent-soft: rgba(122,201,224,0.2);
  --cs-surface: rgba(10,10,20,0.96);
  --cs-text: #c4f0e8;
}

/* ───── GLOBAL BASE ───── */
html, body, .wp-site-blocks {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

html, body { overflow-x: hidden; }

/* ───── DEFAULT TEXT STYLING ───── */
p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: #c4f0e8 !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(196,240,232,.3), 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0.15px rgba(0,0,0,.4);
}

/* ───── ALL HEADERS - PURPLE ───── */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6,

  text-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.4) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0.4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) !important;
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {

  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 1500 !important;
  pointer-events: auto !important;
}

header .wp-block-navigation__container {
  display: flex !important;
  gap: 2rem !important;
  flex-wrap: wrap !important;
  align-items: center !important;
}

/* 2. TOP-LEVEL NAV ITEMS - Always visible */
header .wp-block-navigation-item,
header .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item,
header .wp-block-navigation-submenu {
  display: inline-flex !important;
  position: relative !important;
  pointer-events: auto !important;
}

header .wp-block-navigation-item > a,
header .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
  display: block !important;
  pointer-events: auto !important;
}

/* 3. DROPDOWN CONTAINERS - Hidden by default, show on hover */
header .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  display: none !important;
  position: absolute !important;
  top: 100% !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  background: rgba(11, 11, 15, 0.98) !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.2) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  padding: 10px !important;
  min-width: 250px !important;
  z-index: 2000 !important;
  margin-top: 4px !important;
}

/* Show dropdown on hover/focus */
header .wp-block-navigation-item:hover > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container,
header .wp-block-navigation-item:focus-within > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  display: block !important;
}

/* 4. ITEMS INSIDE DROPDOWNS - Always visible when dropdown is open */
header .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container .wp-block-navigation-item {
  display: block !important;
  width: 100% !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAVIGATION STYLING
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

/* Top nav links - purple, no backgrounds */
.nav-top a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .3rem .6rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.nav-top a:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation svg {
  fill: var(--cs-purple) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EPISODES DROPDOWN - BLACK BACKGROUND
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container,
.episodes-menu > ul {
  background: #000 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  padding: 16px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;
}

.episodes-menu a,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  display: block !important;
}

.episodes-menu a:hover {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.06) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   REGULAR DROPDOWNS (non-episodes)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) {
  background: rgba(10, 14, 20, 0.85) !important;

---

backdrop-filter: blur(12px) !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.25) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  padding: .35rem 0 !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  padding: .45rem .75rem !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EPISODE LINK COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.06) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   FOOTER NAVIGATION
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.footer-links,
footer .footer-links,
.footer-nav,
footer .footer-nav {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

.footer-links a,
.footer-nav a,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.4) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0.4px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3) !important;
  padding: .2rem .4rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  font-size: 0.75rem !important;
}

.footer-links a:hover,
.footer-nav a:hover,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.6) !important;
}

.footer-links {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 10px;
  left: 0;
  z-index: 5000;
  background: rgba(10, 14, 20, 0.85);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.25);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem;
  width: auto;
  display: inline-block;
}

.footer-links ul {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  align-items: flex-start;
}

/* Hide footer links on specific pages */
body.page-id-945 .footer-links,
body.page-id-856 .footer-links,
body.page-id-1336 .footer-links {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Fix footer positioning on FAQs and About pages */
body.page-id-554 .footer-links,
body.page-id-556 .footer-links {
  position: fixed !important;
  bottom: 10px !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  z-index: 5000 !important;
}

/* Remove extra space above the data veil on About and FAQs pages */
body.page-id-554 .cs-veil,
body.page-id-556 .cs-veil {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  padding-top: 0 !important;
}

/* Remove any top spacing on the entire page content */
body.page-id-554 .wp-site-blocks,
body.page-id-556 .wp-site-blocks,
body.page-id-554 main,
body.page-id-556 main {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  padding-top: 0 !important;
}

/* Make navigation buttons flush to left edge on About and FAQs pages */
body.page-id-554 .cs-arc-nav,
body.page-id-556 .cs-arc-nav {
  margin-left: 0 !important;
  padding-left: 0 !important;
}

/* Also fix the root navigation container */
body.page-id-554 .cs-arc-root,
body.page-id-556 .cs-arc-root {
  padding-left: 0 !important;
  margin-left: 0 !important;
}

footer .wp-block-group.alignfull[style*="--wp--preset--spacing--60"],
.wp-block-template-part__footer .wp-block-group.alignfull[style*="--wp--preset--spacing--60"] {
  padding-top: 0 !important;
  padding-bottom: 0.25rem !important;
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}

footer .footer-nav,
.wp-block-template-part__footer .footer-nav {
  margin: 0 auto !important;
  padding-top: 0.25rem !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
  justify-content: center !important;
  gap: 1rem;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "Return\ANode"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: 13px;
  bottom: 13px;
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: pre;
text-align: center;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;

---

opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SCROLLBAR
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  scrollbar-color: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.3) #0b0b0f;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 12px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: #0b0b0f;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.3);
  border-radius: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.5);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PAGE TITLE
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

h1.entry-title,
.wp-block-post-title {
  margin-top: 25px !important;
  margin-bottom: 30px !important;
  text-align: center;
}

.entry-content,
.wp-block-post-content {
  max-width: none !important;
  padding-left: 0 !important;
  padding-right: 0 !important;
}

main > *:last-child,
.wp-block-post-content > *:last-child,
.entry-content > *:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   FULL-WIDTH BLOCKS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.wp-site-blocks .alignfull {
  width: 100vw;
  max-width: 100vw;
  margin-left: calc(50% - 50vw);
  margin-right: calc(50% - 50vw);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   WPFORO FORUM
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.wpforo-wrap,
#wpforo-wrap,
.wpforo-main,
#wpforo .wpforo-container,
#wpforo .wpf-container,
.wpforo-forum,
.wpforo-table,
#wpforo #wpforo-wrap,
.wpforo-subtop,

body.wpforo-page #wpforo-wrap,

  max-width: 100% !important;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CUSTOM STORY NAVIGATION (from your HTML snippet)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.cs-arc-nav { 
  margin: 0.5rem 0 1rem 20px;
  text-align: left;
  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
}

.cs-arc-root {
  list-style: none; 
  padding: 0; 
  margin: 0; 
  display: inline-flex; 
  gap: 1.5rem;
  justify-content: flex-start;
}

.cs-arc-parent { 
  position: relative;
}

.cs-arc-toggle {
  background: transparent;
  border: 1px solid var(--cs-accent-soft);
  color: var(--cs-accent);
  padding: .4rem .9rem;
  border-radius: 10px;
  letter-spacing: .02em;
  cursor: pointer;
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px color-mix(in oklab, var(--cs-accent) 60%, transparent);

---

}

.cs-arc-toggle:hover,
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] {
  border-color: var(--cs-accent);
  box-shadow: 0 0 12px color-mix(in oklab, var(--cs-accent) 45%, transparent);
}

.cs-arc-sub {
  display: none;
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0; 
  padding: .6rem .9rem;
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: calc(100% + .4rem);
  transform: none;
  min-width: 280px;
  background: var(--cs-surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--cs-accent-soft);
  border-radius: 12px;
  text-align: left;
  z-index: 3000;
}

.cs-arc-parent:hover .cs-arc-sub,
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] + .cs-arc-sub {
  display: block;
}

.cs-arc-sub li { 
  margin: .15rem 0; 
}

.cs-arc-sub a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  display: block;
  padding: .35rem .5rem;
  border-radius: 6px;
  transition: background 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease;
}

.cs-arc-sub a:hover {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.08) !important;
  color: var(--mint) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTIC SYNDICATE — DATA VEIL RESTORE
   Non-destructive patch for glyph scroll + rotating line
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  --veil-bg: rgba(6,10,22,0.82);
  --veil-fg: #e6ff6a;
  --veil-accent: #99e9ff;
  --veil-speed: 55s;
}

/* overall wrapper */
/* VEIL CONTAINER */
.cs-veil {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 1200 !important;      /* below nav so menus are clickable */
  background: var(--veil-bg);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  overflow: hidden;
  text-align: center;
  padding: .35rem 0 .4rem;

/* remove old border */
  border: none !important;

/* arrange for top & bottom dot lines */
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;

pointer-events: none !important;
}
.cs-veil * { pointer-events: auto; }

/* SCROLLING GLYPH LINE */
.cs-veil-symbols {
  display: inline-block;
  white-space: nowrap;
  padding-left: 0;  /* start visible */
  color: var(--veil-fg);
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem rgba(230,255,106,.6);
  letter-spacing: .18rem;
  font-size: clamp(13px, 2.6vw, 18px);
  animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite;
  animation-delay: -20s;
}

/* ROTATING STATUS LINE */
#cs-veil-line {
  display: block;
  margin-top: .25rem;
  color: var(--veil-accent);
  font-family: ui-monospace, "JetBrains Mono", Consolas, monospace;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
}

/* BLUE GLOWING DOT ROWS */
.cs-dotrow {
  height: 2px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(
    rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.9) 40%, transparent 42%
  );
  background-size: 10px 2px;
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  opacity: 0.9;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.6));
}

/* TOP DOT LINE (added via pseudo-element) */
.cs-veil::before {
  content: "";
  display: block;
  height: 2px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(
    rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.9) 40%, transparent 42%
  );
  background-size: 10px 2px;
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  opacity: 0.9;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.6));
}

/* MOTION SAFETY */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .cs-veil-symbols { animation: none !important; }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DATA VEIL — INTERACTION + SPEED FIX
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* allow clicks to pass through the banner except its text */
.cs-veil {
  pointer-events: none !important;     /* banner itself doesn’t block clicks */
}
.cs-veil * {
  pointer-events: auto;                /* but its inner text still receives hover/glow etc */
}

```css
:root{

.article-content a,

}

/* ── Footer simple links: keep on-brand ── */

}
```

[1760653244.457342] Assistant: **TL;DR:** Here’s a drop-in **reader-mode toggle** that only flips **body text** and **in-content links**; your **brand purple** stays untouched.

### Plan (pseudocode)
- Add two tokens: `--text-main`, `--link-body`.
- Keep current “dark” values; define “light” overrides under `html[data-read-theme="light"]`.
- Style a small fixed button near your Return node.
- JS: read saved mode from `localStorage`, set `data-read-theme`, sync `aria-pressed`, toggle on click.

```html
<!-- ===================== READ MODE TOGGLE =====================
Place this in a site-wide template (Header/Footer) or a "Custom HTML" block.
It ONLY changes body text + in-content link colors. Brand purple remains.
================================================================ -->

<!-- Toggle Button -->
<button id="cs-read-toggle"
        class="cs-read-toggle"
        type="button"
        aria-pressed="false"
        title="Toggle reading theme">
  <span class="cs-read-icon" aria-hidden="true">☾</span>
  <span class="cs-read-label">Reader</span>
</button>

<style>
/* ===== CSS: put this AFTER your current CSS (very end) ===== */

/* Tokens already exist; define light-mode overrides by data-attr */
html[data-read-theme="light"]{
  /* brighter body text + cooler links; hover still brand-hover */
  --text-main: #EAF6FF;
  --link-body: #A9C6FF;
}

/* Body text + content links already consume these vars;
   ensure they respond when vars change */
p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: var(--text-main) !important;
}
.entry-content a,
.wp-block-post-content a,
.article-content a,
main a:not(.wp-block-navigation-item__content):not(.wp-block-button__link),
.site-main a:not(nav a) {
  color: var(--link-body) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-body) 60%, transparent);
}
.entry-content a:hover,
.wp-block-post-content a:hover,
.article-content a:hover,
main a:not(.wp-block-navigation-item__content):not(.wp-block-button__link):hover,
.site-main a:not(nav a):hover {
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important; /* #7EFBB3 from your override */
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 80%, transparent);
}

/* Toggle button styling (kept tiny, avoids Return button) */
.cs-read-toggle{
  position: fixed;
  right: 86px;               /* leaves space for .cs-return at 13px */
  bottom: 14px;
  z-index: 5200;
  padding: .45rem .7rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover, #7EFBB3) 40%, transparent);
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.55);
  color: var(--brand-hover, #7EFBB3);
  font: 600 .72rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .06em;
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: transform .15s ease, box-shadow .15s ease, border-color .15s ease;
}
.cs-read-toggle:hover{ transform: translateY(-1px); box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(126,251,179,.25); }
.cs-read-toggle:active{ transform: translateY(0); }
.cs-read-toggle[aria-pressed="true"] .cs-read-icon{ content: "☀"; }
.cs-read-icon{ margin-right: .35rem; }

/* Mobile nudge */
@media (max-width: 768px){
  .cs-read-toggle{ right: 78px; bottom: 12px; padding: .38rem .6rem; font-size: .68rem; }
}
</style>

<script>
/* ===== JS: persistence + toggle ===== */
(() => {
  const KEY = "cs-read-theme";                 // localStorage key
  const root = document.documentElement;
  const btn  = document.getElementById("cs-read-toggle");
  if (!btn) return;

// init from storage
  const saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY);     // "light" | "dark" | null
  if (saved === "light") {
    root.setAttribute("data-read-theme", "light");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
    btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☀";
  }

// toggle handler
  btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isLight = root.getAttribute("data-read-theme") === "light";
    if (isLight) {
      root.removeAttribute("data-read-theme");
      localStorage.setItem(KEY, "dark");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
      btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☾";
    } else {
      root.setAttribute("data-read-theme", "light");
      localStorage.setItem(KEY, "light");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
      btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☀";
    }
  });
})();
</script>
```

```css
/* Body text + content links consume the tokens */
p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption { color: var(--text-main) !important; }

  color: var(--link-body) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-body) 60%, transparent);
}
.entry-content a:hover,
.wp-block-post-content a:hover,
.article-content a:hover,
main a:not(.wp-block-navigation-item__content):not(.wp-block-button__link):hover,
.site-main a:not(nav a):hover {
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 80%, transparent);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent);
}

/* Keep brand purple on headings/menus */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, .wp-block-post-title, .entry-title { color: var(--brand-purple) !important; }
header .wp-block-navigation a,
.nav-top a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a { color: var(--brand-purple) !important; }
header .wp-block-navigation a:hover,
.nav-top a:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover { color: var(--brand-hover) !important; }
.episodes-menu a,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a { color: var(--brand-purple) !important; }
.episodes-menu a:hover,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover { color: var(--brand-hover) !important; }

/* Rotating header status line */
#cs-veil-line { color: var(--brand-hover) !important; }

/* Reader toggle button styles */
.cs-read-toggle{
  position: fixed;
  right: 86px;
  bottom: 14px;
  z-index: 5200;
  padding: .45rem .7rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 40%, transparent);
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.55);
  color: var(--brand-hover);
  font: 600 .72rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .06em;
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: transform .15s ease, box-shadow .15s ease, border-color .15s ease;
}
.cs-read-toggle:hover{ transform: translateY(-1px); box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(126,251,179,.25); }
.cs-read-icon{ margin-right: .35rem; }

@media (max-width: 768px){
  .cs-read-toggle{ right: 78px; bottom: 12px; padding: .38rem .6rem; font-size: .68rem; }
}
```

---

### 2) HTML — add via **Site Editor → (Header or Footer) → Add block → “Custom HTML”**

```html
<button id="cs-read-toggle" class="cs-read-toggle" type="button" aria-pressed="false" title="Toggle reading theme">
  <span class="cs-read-icon" aria-hidden="true">☾</span>
  <span class="cs-read-label">Reader</span>
</button>
```

---

### 3) JS — add with a **Code Snippets** plugin, or in your theme’s footer scripts

```html
<script>
(() => {
  const KEY = "cs-read-theme";
  const root = document.documentElement;
  const btn  = document.getElementById("cs-read-toggle");
  if (!btn) return;

const saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
  if (saved === "light") {
    root.setAttribute("data-read-theme", "light");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
    btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☀";
  }

btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isLight = root.getAttribute("data-read-theme") === "light";
    if (isLight) {
      root.removeAttribute("data-read-theme");
      localStorage.setItem(KEY, "dark");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
      btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☾";
    } else {
      root.setAttribute("data-read-theme", "light");
      localStorage.setItem(KEY, "light");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
      btn.querySelector(".cs-read-icon").textContent = "☀";
    }
  });
})();
</script>
```

---

### Common reasons WP says “error” in Additional CSS
- You pasted **`<style>…</style>`** or **`<script>`** or HTML — remove them.
- A comment `/* …` wasn’t closed with `*/`.
- Pasted **smart quotes** from a notes app. Replace with straight quotes.
- A stray `}` or missing `}`. (Use the blocks above exactly.)

[1760653776.705978] Assistant: **TL;DR:** Keep purple where it signals structure (menus/headings), sprinkle the mint **#7EFBB3** into *micro-accents* so it guides the eye without shouting: selection, markers, rules, blockquotes, focus rings, active nav, subtle heading underlines.

### Plan (pseudocode)
- Use mint for small UI feedback: selection, focus, caret.
- Add mint to list bullets via `::marker`.
- Add thin mint underline accent to headings (after pseudo).
- Make `<hr>` and blockquote edge glow mint.
- Highlight “current” menu item in mint.
- Keep body links the blue/periwinkle you chose.

```css
/* ==== MINT MICRO-ACCENTS — paste at very end ==== */
:root{
  --brand-purple: #BF40BF;
  --brand-hover:  #7EFBB3; /* mint accent */
}

/* 1) Text selection + inputs: gentle mint feedback */
::selection{ background: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 28%, transparent); color:#001a10; }

---

input, textarea { caret-color: var(--brand-hover); }
a:focus, button:focus, [tabindex]:focus {
  outline: 2px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 70%, transparent) !important;
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-radius: 6px;
}

/* 2) Lists: mint bullets only (keeps link color intact) */
ul li::marker{ color: var(--brand-hover); }

/* 3) Headings: subtle mint underline accent bar */
h2, h3 {
  position: relative;
}
h2::after, h3::after{
  content:"";
  position:absolute;
  left:50%; transform:translateX(-50%);
  bottom:-0.45rem;
  width: 64%;
  height: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg,
    transparent 0%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 65%, transparent) 18%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 85%, transparent) 50%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 65%, transparent) 82%,
    transparent 100%);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent));
  opacity:.9;
}

/* 4) Horizontal rules: mint glow separators */
hr{
  border:0; height:2px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 90%, transparent) 45%, transparent 46%) repeat-x;
  background-size: 12px 2px;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent));
  opacity:.85;
}

/* 5) Blockquotes: thin mint edge + soft tint */
blockquote{
  border-left: 3px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 70%, transparent);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 10%, transparent), transparent 35%);
  padding-left: 1rem !important;
}

/* 6) Nav “current” page: show state using mint (not hover) */
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-item > a,
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-ancestor > a{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent) !important;
}

/* 7) Episode menu: add tiny mint dot before each item (subtle) */
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a::before{
  content:"•";
  margin-right:.5ch;
  color: var(--brand-hover);
  opacity:.85;
}

/* 8) Tiny underline tweak: links get a mintier underline on hover only */
.entry-content a:hover,
.wp-block-post-content a:hover{
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 85%, transparent) !important;
  text-underline-offset: 2px;
  text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
}
```

Where this adds mint:
- **Eyes naturally follow** the mint underline on section headings and the dotted `<hr>` between blocks.
- **Reading UX** improves via mint selection/caret/focus rings.
- **Navigation state** (current page) is clear without changing the brand structure.
- **Lists & episode items** gain a small, tasteful mint cue.

<script>
(function(){
  const lines = [
    "[Decrypting field patterns…]",
    "[Resonance threshold passed]",
    "[Echo nodes: online]",
    "[Signal integrity: 99.97%]",
    "[Phase: 7/13 — synchronization in progress]",
    "[Awaiting user attunement…]",
    "[Glitch→Glyph: successful]",
    "[Paradox stabilized]"
  ];
  const el = document.getElementById("cs-veil-line");
  if (!el) return;
  let i = 0;

function glitch() {
    el.style.transform = `translate(${Math.random()*4-2}px, ${Math.random()*2-1}px)`;
    el.style.filter = 'hue-rotate(180deg)';
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.transform = ''; el.style.filter = ''; }, 50);
  }

function swap(){
    if (Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();
    i = (i+1) % lines.length;
    el.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(()=>{ el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
  }
  setInterval(swap, 4200);

const t = document.querySelector('.cs-veil-symbols');
  if (t && !t.dataset.duped) {
    t.textContent = t.textContent + ' ' + t.textContent;
    t.dataset.duped = '1';
  }

setInterval(() => {
    if (t) {
      t.style.animation = 'none';
      void t.offsetWidth;
      t.style.animation = '';
    }
  }, 300000);
})();
</script>

```css
/* ===================== DATA VEIL COLOR OVERRIDES =====================

---

Paste this at the VERY END of Appearance → Customize → Additional CSS
===================================================================== */

:root{
  --veil-bg:    rgba(6,10,22,0.82);
  --veil-glyph: #C3B1E1;   /* symbols line (periwinkle) */
  --veil-line:  #7EFBB3;   /* rotating status text (mint) */
  --veil-dots:  #7EFBB3;   /* dotted rails (mint) */
}

/* Console container (keeps your blur/opacity) */
.cs-veil{
  background: var(--veil-bg) !important;
}

/* Scrolling glyph line */
.cs-veil-symbols{
  color: var(--veil-glyph) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-glyph) 55%, transparent) !important;
}

/* Rotating status line */
#cs-veil-line{
  color: var(--veil-line) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 .75rem color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-line) 55%, transparent) !important;
}

/* Dotted rails (top via ::before is already in your CSS; match color) */
.cs-dotrow,
.cs-veil::before{
  background-image: radial-gradient(color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-dots) 95%, transparent) 40%, transparent 42%) !important;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-dots) 60%, transparent)) !important;
  opacity: .95 !important;
}
```

```html
<!-- ===================== SCRIPT (replace your <script> block) =====================
Removes hue-rotate so #7EFBB3 stays mint during the glitch. 
Paste this in the same spot where your old script was.
=============================================================================== -->
<script>
(function(){
  const lines = [
    "[Decrypting field patterns…]",
    "[Resonance threshold passed]",
    "[Echo nodes: online]",
    "[Signal integrity: 99.97%]",
    "[Phase: 7/13 — synchronization in progress]",
    "[Awaiting user attunement…]",
    "[Glitch→Glyph: successful]",
    "[Paradox stabilized]"
  ];
  const el = document.getElementById("cs-veil-line");
  if (!el) return;
  let i = 0;

function glitch() {
    // keep color stable; add micro-jitter + blur only
    el.style.transform = `translate(${(Math.random()*4-2).toFixed(2)}px, ${(Math.random()*2-1).toFixed(2)}px)`;
    el.style.textShadow = "0 0 10px rgba(126,251,179,.55)"; /* mint glow */
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.transform = ""; el.style.textShadow = ""; }, 60);
  }

function swap(){
    if (Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();
    i = (i+1) % lines.length;
    el.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(() => { el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
  }
  setInterval(swap, 4200);

// Duplicate glyph line for seamless marquee
  const t = document.querySelector('.cs-veil-symbols');
  if (t && !t.dataset.duped) {
    t.textContent = t.textContent + ' ' + t.textContent;
    t.dataset.duped = '1';
  }

// Periodic animation reset (prevents stalling)
  setInterval(() => {
    if (!t) return;
    t.style.animation = 'none';
    void t.offsetWidth;
    t.style.animation = '';
  }, 300000);
})();
</script>
```

```css
/* 5) Current/active menu item state → mint (optional) */
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-item > a,
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-ancestor > a{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
}

/* 6) Keep the rotating status line mint */
#cs-veil-line{ color: var(--brand-hover) !important; }
```

```css
/* ── TOP NAV: default mint ── */

/* Hover/Focus/Current → purple */

/* ── SUBMENUS (non-episodes) ── */

/* ── EPISODE dropdown links ── */
.episodes-menu a,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a{

.wp-block-post-title:hover{

}
```

<script>
(function(){
  const lines = [
    "[Decrypting field patterns…]",
    "[Resonance threshold passed]",
    "[Echo nodes: online]",
    "[Signal integrity: 99.97%]",
    "[Phase: 7/13 — synchronization in progress]",
    "[Awaiting user attunement…]",
    "[Glitch→Glyph: successful]",
    "[Paradox stabilized]"
  ];
  const el = document.getElementById("cs-veil-line");
  if (!el) return;
  let i = 0;

function glitch() {
    // keep color stable; add micro-jitter + blur only
    el.style.transform = `translate(${(Math.random()*4-2).toFixed(2)}px, ${(Math.random()*2-1).toFixed(2)}px)`;
    el.style.textShadow = "0 0 10px rgba(126,251,179,.55)"; /* mint glow */
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.transform = ""; el.style.textShadow = ""; }, 60);
  }

function swap(){
    if (Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();
    i = (i+1) % lines.length;
    el.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(() => { el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
  }
  setInterval(swap, 4200);

// Duplicate glyph line for seamless marquee
  const t = document.querySelector('.cs-veil-symbols');
  if (t && !t.dataset.duped) {
    t.textContent = t.textContent + ' ' + t.textContent;
    t.dataset.duped = '1';
  }

// Periodic animation reset (prevents stalling)
  setInterval(() => {
    if (!t) return;
    t.style.animation = 'none';
    void t.offsetWidth;
    t.style.animation = '';
  }, 300000);
})();
</script>

/* ───── COLOR TOKENS ───── */
:root {
  --mint: #c4f0e8;
  --mint-strong: #8fffe6;
  --mint-glow: rgba(196,240,232,.4);
  --rose: #ffc7e6;
  --gold: #ffe6a3;
  --indigo: #c5c9ff;
  --ice: #e7f7ff;

--cs-purple: #7ac9e0;
  --cs-purple-hover: #99e9ff;

--glass: rgba(10,14,20,.65);
  --glass-hover: rgba(10,14,20,.78);
  --mint-border: rgba(169,255,232,.5);
  --stroke: rgba(0,0,0,.4);
  --ink: rgba(0,0,0,.5);
  --glow: rgba(255,255,255,.18);

--cs-accent: #7ac9e0;
  --cs-accent-soft: rgba(122,201,224,0.2);
  --cs-surface: rgba(10,10,20,0.96);
  --cs-text: #c4f0e8;
}

/* ───── GLOBAL BASE ───── */
html, body, .wp-site-blocks {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

html, body { overflow-x: hidden; }

/* ───── DEFAULT TEXT STYLING ───── */
p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: #c4f0e8 !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(196,240,232,.3), 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0.15px rgba(0,0,0,.4);
}

/* ───── ALL HEADERS - PURPLE ───── */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6,
.wp-block-post-title,
.entry-title {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.4) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0.4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) !important;
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.45);
}
a:hover, a:focus {
  color: var(--mint-strong) !important;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.75);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--mint-glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/* 1. FORCE HEADER NAVIGATION VISIBILITY */

.wp-block-template-part__footer .footer-nav {
  margin: 0 auto !important;
  padding-top: 0.25rem !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
  justify-content: center !important;
  gap: 1rem;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "Return\ANode"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: 13px;
  bottom: 13px;
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: pre;
text-align: center;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;
    opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SCROLLBAR
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  scrollbar-color: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.3) #0b0b0f;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 12px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: #0b0b0f;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.3);
  border-radius: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.5);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PAGE TITLE
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

h1.entry-title,
.wp-block-post-title {
  margin-top: 25px !important;
  margin-bottom: 30px !important;
  text-align: center;
}

.entry-content,
.wp-block-post-content {
  max-width: none !important;
  padding-left: 0 !important;
  padding-right: 0 !important;
}

main > *:last-child,
.wp-block-post-content > *:last-child,
.entry-content > *:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   FULL-WIDTH BLOCKS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.wp-site-blocks .alignfull {
  width: 100vw;
  max-width: 100vw;
  margin-left: calc(50% - 50vw);
  margin-right: calc(50% - 50vw);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

.wpforo-wrap,

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CUSTOM STORY NAVIGATION (from your HTML snippet)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.cs-arc-nav { 
  margin: 0.5rem 0 1rem 20px;
  text-align: left;
  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
}

.cs-arc-root {
  list-style: none; 
  padding: 0; 
  margin: 0; 
  display: inline-flex; 
  gap: 1.5rem;
  justify-content: flex-start;
}

.cs-arc-parent { 
  position: relative;
}

.cs-arc-toggle {
  background: transparent;
  border: 1px solid var(--cs-accent-soft);
  color: var(--cs-accent);
  padding: .4rem .9rem;
  border-radius: 10px;
  letter-spacing: .02em;
  cursor: pointer;
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px color-mix(in oklab, var(--cs-accent) 60%, transparent);
}

.cs-arc-toggle:hover,
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] {
  border-color: var(--cs-accent);
  box-shadow: 0 0 12px color-mix(in oklab, var(--cs-accent) 45%, transparent);
}

.cs-arc-sub {
  display: none;
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0; 
  padding: .6rem .9rem;
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: calc(100% + .4rem);
  transform: none;
  min-width: 280px;
  background: var(--cs-surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--cs-accent-soft);
  border-radius: 12px;
  text-align: left;
  z-index: 3000;
}

.cs-arc-parent:hover .cs-arc-sub,
.cs-arc-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] + .cs-arc-sub {
  display: block;
}

.cs-arc-sub li { 
  margin: .15rem 0; 
}

.cs-arc-sub a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  display: block;
  padding: .35rem .5rem;
  border-radius: 6px;
  transition: background 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease;
}

.cs-arc-sub a:hover {
  background: rgba(169, 255, 232, 0.08) !important;
  color: var(--mint) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTIC SYNDICATE — DATA VEIL RESTORE
   Non-destructive patch for glyph scroll + rotating line
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  --veil-bg: rgba(6,10,22,0.82);
  --veil-fg: #e6ff6a;
  --veil-accent: #99e9ff;
  --veil-speed: 55s;
}

/* overall wrapper */
/* VEIL CONTAINER */
.cs-veil {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 1200 !important;      /* below nav so menus are clickable */
  background: var(--veil-bg);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  overflow: hidden;
  text-align: center;
  padding: .35rem 0 .4rem;

/* remove old border */
  border: none !important;

/* arrange for top & bottom dot lines */
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;

pointer-events: none !important;
}
.cs-veil * { pointer-events: auto; }

/* SCROLLING GLYPH LINE */
.cs-veil-symbols {
  display: inline-block;
  white-space: nowrap;
  padding-left: 0;  /* start visible */
  color: var(--veil-fg);
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem rgba(230,255,106,.6);
  letter-spacing: .18rem;
  font-size: clamp(13px, 2.6vw, 18px);
  animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite;
  animation-delay: -20s;
}

/* ROTATING STATUS LINE */
#cs-veil-line {
  display: block;
  margin-top: .25rem;
  color: var(--veil-accent);
  font-family: ui-monospace, "JetBrains Mono", Consolas, monospace;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
}

/* BLUE GLOWING DOT ROWS */
.cs-dotrow {
  height: 2px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(
    rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.9) 40%, transparent 42%
  );
  background-size: 10px 2px;
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  opacity: 0.9;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.6));
}

/* TOP DOT LINE (added via pseudo-element) */
.cs-veil::before {
  content: "";
  display: block;
  height: 2px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(
    rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.9) 40%, transparent 42%
  );
  background-size: 10px 2px;
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  opacity: 0.9;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.6));
}

/* MOTION SAFETY */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .cs-veil-symbols { animation: none !important; }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DATA VEIL — INTERACTION + SPEED FIX
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* allow clicks to pass through the banner except its text */

---

.cs-veil {
  pointer-events: none !important;     /* banner itself doesn’t block clicks */
}
.cs-veil * {
  pointer-events: auto;                /* but its inner text still receives hover/glow etc */
}

/* slow down glyph speed (increase duration) */
.cs-veil-symbols {
  animation-duration: 33s !important; /* was 55s — now slower and smoother */
}

/* ensure it never covers dropdowns visually */
.cs-veil {
  z-index: 1200 !important;            /* below your .cs-arc-nav (2000) and WP nav (1500) */
}

.cs-veil-symbols {
  display: inline-block;
  white-space: nowrap;
  padding-left: 0;  /* start visible */
  color: var(--veil-fg);
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem rgba(230,255,106,.6);
  letter-spacing: .18rem;
  font-size: clamp(13px, 2.6vw, 18px);
  animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite;
  animation-delay: -20s; /* starts partway through the cycle */
}

@keyframes cs-marquee {
  from { transform: translateX(0); }
  to   { transform: translateX(-50%); }
}

.cs-dotrow {
  height: 2px; /* thicker line */
  margin-top: .4rem;
  background-image: radial-gradient(
    rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.9) 40%,   /* bright light-blue */
    transparent 42%
  );
  background-size: 10px 2px;        /* bigger dots, more space between */
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  opacity: 0.9;                     /* more visible but not overpowering */
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(153, 233, 255, 0.6)); /* gentle glow */
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CONTENT VISIBILITY FIX
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Make sure content area is visible */
main,
article,
.entry-content,
.wp-block-post-content,
.wp-site-blocks > * {
  background: transparent !important;
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

/* Ensure all blocks within content are visible */
.entry-content > *,
.wp-block-post-content > * {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

/* Make sure images show up */
img,
figure,
.wp-block-image {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
  display: block !important;
}

/* Ensure text blocks are visible */
.wp-block-paragraph,
.wp-block-heading,
.wp-block-group {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
}

/* Aggressive gap removal */
.cs-veil {
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}
.wp-site-blocks > * {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}
h1.entry-title,
.wp-block-post-title {
  margin-top: -55px !important;
  padding-top: 0 !important;
  margin-bottom: 30px !important;
}

.footer-links {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 10px;
  left: 0;  /* Flush to left */
  z-index: 5000;
  background: rgba(10, 14, 20, 0.85);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.25);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 0.6rem 1rem;
  width: auto;
  display: inline-block;
}

/* Mobile adjustments */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .footer-links {
    padding: 0.3rem 0.5rem !important; /* Much smaller container */
    border-radius: 8px !important; /* Smaller border radius */
  }
}

.footer-links ul {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: row;
  gap: 1.5rem;
  align-items: center;
}

/* Mobile ul adjustments */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .footer-links ul {
    gap: 0.5rem !important; /* Tighter spacing on mobile */
  }
}

.footer-links ul {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: row;
  gap: 1.5rem;
  align-items: center;
}

/* Mobile adjustments */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .footer-links {
    padding: 0.2rem 0.3rem !important; /* Even smaller container */
    border-radius: 6px !important; /* Smaller border radius */
  }

.footer-links a,
  .footer-nav a,
  .footer-links .wp-block-button__link,
  .footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
  .footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content a {
    padding: .1rem .2rem !important; /* Much smaller padding */
    font-size: 0.6rem !important; /* Even smaller text */
  }

.footer-links ul {
    gap: 0.3rem !important; /* Very tight spacing */
  }
}

/* Hide footer links on Brayne Snax and Mr. E pages, keep only return node */
body.page-id-945 .footer-links,
body.page-id-856 .footer-links {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Hide footer links on Glyph Graffiti Wall page */
body.page-id-1336 .footer-links {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Fix footer positioning on FAQs and About pages */
body.page-id-554 .footer-links,
body.page-id-556 .footer-links {
  position: fixed !important;
  bottom: 10px !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  z-index: 5000 !important;
}

/* Remove extra space above the data veil on About and FAQs pages */
body.page-id-554 .cs-veil,
body.page-id-556 .cs-veil {

---

margin-top: 0 !important;
  padding-top: 0 !important;
}

/* Remove any top spacing on the entire page content */
body.page-id-554 .wp-site-blocks,
body.page-id-556 .wp-site-blocks,
body.page-id-554 main,
body.page-id-556 main {
  margin-top: 0 !important;

}

/* Make navigation buttons flush to left edge on About and FAQs pages */

body.page-id-556 .cs-arc-nav {

  padding-left: 0 !important;

body.page-id-556 .cs-arc-root {
  padding-left: 0 !important;
  margin-left: 0 !important;
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {
  color: var(--cs-accent) !important; /* Changed from var(--mint) to var(--cs-accent) for purple links */
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(183,166,255,.45); /* Updated underline color to match */
}
a:hover, a:focus {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important; /* Changed hover color */
  text-decoration-color: rgba(183,166,255,.75); /* Updated hover underline */
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(183,166,255,.4); /* Updated glow effect */
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BASE44 IFRAME MOBILE FIXES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Make iframe more touch-responsive */
iframe[src*="base44"] {
  touch-action: manipulation;
  pointer-events: auto;
  -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
}

/* Mobile iframe styling */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  iframe[src*="base44"] {
    width: 100% !important;
    min-height: 70vh;
    border: none;
  }
}

/* Mobile overlay styling */
.mobile-iframe-overlay {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: white;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  z-index: 10;
  pointer-events: none;
}

@media (min-width: 769px) {
  .mobile-iframe-overlay {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* Additional mobile iframe fixes */
iframe[src*="base44"] {
  pointer-events: auto !important;
  -webkit-touch-callout: default;
  -webkit-user-select: auto;

---

user-select: auto;
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  iframe[src*="base44"] {
    z-index: 999;
    position: relative;
  }
}

}

  white-space: nowrap !important;
  animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite !important;
  animation-delay: -20s !important;
  animation-play-state: running !important;
  will-change: transform !important;
  color: #e6ff6a !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
  opacity: 1 !important;
}

/* Force glyph animation on Mr. E and Brayne Snax pages - ULTIMATE FIX */
body.page-id-945 .cs-veil,
body.page-id-856 .cs-veil {
  display: flex !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
  opacity: 1 !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  position: sticky !important;
  top: 0 !important;
}

body.page-id-945 .cs-veil-symbols,
body.page-id-856 .cs-veil-symbols {
  display: inline-block !important;
  white-space: nowrap !important;
  animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite !important;
  animation-delay: -20s !important;
  animation-play-state: running !important;
  will-change: transform !important;
  color: #e6ff6a !important;
  visibility: visible !important;
  opacity: 1 !important;
}

/* SPECIAL FIX ONLY FOR PAGE 856 (Mr. E) - REMOVED TRANSFORM LOCK */
body.page-id-856 .cs-veil-symbols {
  animation-name: cs-marquee !important;
  animation-duration: 33s !important;
  animation-timing-function: linear !important;
  animation-iteration-count: infinite !important;
  animation-delay: -20s !important;
  /* Removed transform: translateX(0) - let animation control it! */
}

/* Make absolutely sure animations aren't disabled by prefers-reduced-motion on these pages */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  body.page-id-945 .cs-veil-symbols,
  body.page-id-856 .cs-veil-symbols {
    animation: cs-marquee 33s linear infinite !important;
  }
}

/* ===== COLOR TWEAKS — paste at the VERY end ===== */

/* Core brand stays the same */
:root{
  --brand-purple: #BF40BF;   /* menus, episode links, etc. */
  --brand-hover:  #7EFBB3;   /* hover + rotating status line */
  --text-main:    #99E9FF;   /* body text */

/* Headings = the periwinkle you liked */
  --heading:      #C3B1E1;

/* ===== CHOOSE ONE for in-page link color ===== */
  /* Option A — darker blue (default) */
  --link-body:    #5B8CFF;

/* Option B — mint green (uncomment to use) */
  /* --link-body:    #7EFBB3; */
}

/* Headings (all levels & post titles) -> periwinkle */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6,
.wp-block-post-title,
.entry-title{
  color: var(--heading) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px color-mix(in oklab, var(--heading) 35%, transparent) !important;
}

/* Body text remains light blue */
p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption{
  color: var(--text-main) !important;
}

/* In-page content links (NOT menus) */
.entry-content a,
.wp-block-post-content a,
.article-content a,
main a:not(.wp-block-navigation-item__content):not(.wp-block-button__link),
.site-main a:not(nav a){
  color: var(--link-body) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--link-body) 60%, transparent);
}
.entry-content a:hover,
.wp-block-post-content a:hover,
.article-content a:hover,
main a:not(.wp-block-navigation-item__content):not(.wp-block-button__link):hover,
.site-main a:not(nav a):hover{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important; /* consistent, readable hover */
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 80%, transparent);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent);
}

/* Menus/episodes stay brand purple + mint hover */
header .wp-block-navigation a,
.nav-top a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a{
  color: var(--brand-purple) !important;
}
header .wp-block-navigation a:hover,
.nav-top a:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
}
.episodes-menu a,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a{
  color: var(--brand-purple) !important;
}
.episodes-menu a:hover,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
}

/* Rotating header line stays mint */

---

#cs-veil-line{ color: var(--brand-hover) !important; }

/* ==== MINT MICRO-ACCENTS — paste at very end ==== */
:root{
  --brand-purple: #BF40BF;
  --brand-hover:  #7EFBB3; /* mint accent */
}

/* 1) Text selection + inputs: gentle mint feedback */
::selection{ background: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 28%, transparent); color:#001a10; }
input, textarea { caret-color: var(--brand-hover); }
a:focus, button:focus, [tabindex]:focus {
  outline: 2px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 70%, transparent) !important;
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-radius: 6px;
}

/* 2) Lists: mint bullets only (keeps link color intact) */
ul li::marker{ color: var(--brand-hover); }

/* 3) Headings: subtle mint underline accent bar */
h2, h3 {
  position: relative;
}
h2::after, h3::after{
  content:"";
  position:absolute;
  left:50%; transform:translateX(-50%);
  bottom:-0.45rem;
  width: 64%;
  height: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg,
    transparent 0%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 65%, transparent) 18%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 85%, transparent) 50%,
    color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 65%, transparent) 82%,
    transparent 100%);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent));
  opacity:.9;
}

/* 4) Horizontal rules: mint glow separators */
hr{
  border:0; height:2px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 90%, transparent) 45%, transparent 46%) repeat-x;
  background-size: 12px 2px;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent));
  opacity:.85;
}

/* 5) Blockquotes: thin mint edge + soft tint */
blockquote{
  border-left: 3px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 70%, transparent);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 10%, transparent), transparent 35%);
  padding-left: 1rem !important;
}

/* 6) Nav “current” page: show state using mint (not hover) */
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-item > a,
header .wp-block-navigation .current-menu-ancestor > a{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 45%, transparent) !important;
}

/* 7) Episode menu: add tiny mint dot before each item (subtle) */
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a::before{
  content:"•";
  margin-right:.5ch;
  color: var(--brand-hover);
  opacity:.85;
}

/* 8) Tiny underline tweak: links get a mintier underline on hover only */
.entry-content a:hover,
.wp-block-post-content a:hover{
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--brand-hover) 85%, transparent) !important;
  text-underline-offset: 2px;
  text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
}

/* ===================== DATA VEIL COLOR OVERRIDES =====================
Paste this at the VERY END of Appearance → Customize → Additional CSS
===================================================================== */

:root{
  --veil-bg:    rgba(6,10,22,0.82);
  --veil-glyph: #C3B1E1;   /* symbols line (periwinkle) */
  --veil-line:  #7EFBB3;   /* rotating status text (mint) */
  --veil-dots:  #7EFBB3;   /* dotted rails (mint) */
}

/* Console container (keeps your blur/opacity) */
.cs-veil{
  background: var(--veil-bg) !important;
}

/* Scrolling glyph line */
.cs-veil-symbols{
  color: var(--veil-glyph) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-glyph) 55%, transparent) !important;
}

/* Rotating status line */
#cs-veil-line{
  color: var(--veil-line) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 .75rem color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-line) 55%, transparent) !important;
}

/* Dotted rails (top via ::before is already in your CSS; match color) */
.cs-dotrow,
.cs-veil::before{
  background-image: radial-gradient(color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-dots) 95%, transparent) 40%, transparent 42%) !important;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-dots) 60%, transparent)) !important;
  opacity: .95 !important;
}

/* ===== FIX: Menu links purple + glyphs back to gold ===== */
/* Put this at the VERY END of Additional CSS */

/* Brand tokens */
:root{
  --brand-purple: #BF40BF;
  --brand-hover:  #7EFBB3;
  --veil-glyph:   #E6FF6A;   /* gold symbols */
}

/* 1) Scrolling glyphs → gold again */
.cs-veil-symbols{
  color: var(--veil-glyph) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 .6rem color-mix(in oklab, var(--veil-glyph) 55%, transparent) !important;
}

/* 2) TOP-LEVEL MENU LINKS (anchors/ancient/etc.) → purple */
header .wp-block-navigation a,
header .wp-block-navigation .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a{
  color: var(--brand-purple) !important;     /* break inheritance (was blue) */
  text-decoration: none !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* Hover/focus → mint */
header .wp-block-navigation a:hover,
header .wp-block-navigation a:focus,
.nav-top a:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
}

/* Menu icons */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation svg{ fill: var(--brand-purple) !important; }

/* 3) SUBMENUS (non-episodes) → purple, hover mint */
.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a{
  color: var(--brand-purple) !important;
}
.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a:hover{
  color: var(--brand-hover) !important;
}

/* 4) EPISODE DROPDOWN LINKS → purple, hover mint */
